Pact Consumer Contract（docs/blueprint/contracts/pact/*.json） 4. IaC 模板（docs/blueprint/infra/helm/values.yaml 或 docker-compose.yml + network-policy） 5. Seed Code Skeleton（docs/blueprint/scaffold/src/） 呼叫時機：gendoc-flow CONTRACTS 步驟（所有文件完成後）；或用戶手動執行 ([ibalasite/gendoc](https://github.com/ibalasite/gendoc/tree/HEAD/skills/gendoc-gen-contracts))
- **gendoc-gen-diagrams**: 從 EDD/ARCH/API/PRD 提取全部 9 種 Server UML 圖，並（條件執行） 從 FRONTEND.md/PDD.md/VDD.md 生成 16 種 Frontend UML 圖， 輸出至 docs/diagrams/（server-: use-case/class×3/object/sequence×N/comm/state×N/activity×N/component/deployment/er-diagram； frontend-: use-case/class×3/object/sequence×3/state×2/activity×3/component/deployment/communication）。 另輸出 class-inventory.md（class→test file 對應表）。 conditions: docs/CICD.md 存在時額外生成 Step 2D — 5 張 CI/CD UML 圖（cicd-pipeline-sequence / cicd-pr-gate-activity / infra-local-topology / developer-workflow-activity / cicd-secret-flow）。永遠生成 Step 2E-MODULITH — 2 張 Spring Modulith 圖（moduli… ([ibalasite/gendoc](https://github.com/ibalasite/gendoc/tree/HEAD/skills/gendoc-gen-diagrams))
- **gendoc-gen-html**: 將 docs/*.md 和 features/*.feature 動態掃描並一對一轉換為專業 HTML 文件網站（docs/pages/）。 Mermaid 圖表直接渲染，深色頂導覽列 + 淺色內容區 + 中文字型，Prism.js 語法高亮，RWD。 Mode 1（預設）：全產生 — README.md + HTML；Mode 2：僅 HTML。 可獨立呼叫或由 gendoc-auto 自動呼叫。 ([ibalasite/gendoc](https://github.com/ibalasite/gendoc/tree/HEAD/skills/gendoc-gen-html))
- **gendoc-gen-mock**: 根據 docs/API.md 與 docs/SCHEMA.md 生成 FastAPI Mock Server。 輸出至 docs/blueprint/mock/（可整個目錄帶走）。 包含：main.py（所有 endpoint 1:1 對應 API.md）、requirements.txt、 data/*.json（擬真假資料）、MOCK_SERVER_GUIDE.md（前端工程師使用手冊）。 呼叫時機：gendoc-flow MOCK 步驟；或用戶手動執行。 condition: client_type != api-only ([ibalasite/gendoc](https://github.com/ibalasite/gendoc/tree/HEAD/skills/gendoc-gen-mock))
- **gendoc-gen-prototype**: 從工程文件自動生成互動式 HTML Prototype，輸出至 docs/pages/prototype/。支援兩種模式： UI Prototype（PRD/PDD/VDD/FRONTEND/AUDIO/ANIM → 可點擊畫面 + 動畫音效） API Explorer（API.md/SCHEMA.md → Postman 式試打介面，JavaScript 模擬回應，含 deep-link 分享） 兩種文件同時存在時並行生成（full 模式）。含 gen→review→fix loop，commit，並自動更新 README.md。 可獨立呼叫（/gendoc-gen-prototype）或由 gendoc-gen-html 自動呼叫。 ([ibalasite/gendoc](https://github.com/ibalasite/gendoc/tree/HEAD/skills/gendoc-gen-prototype))
- **gendoc-guard**: 監控任意 skill 執行，session 中斷時自動排隊重新喚起，並透過 SECS 白名單攔截未授權工具呼叫。用法：/gendoc-guard &lt;skill-name&gt; ([ibalasite/gendoc](https://github.com/ibalasite/gendoc/tree/HEAD/skills/gendoc-guard))
- **gendoc-rebuild-templates**: 全量重建 templates/ 目錄下所有 *.review.md（審查標準）、驗證 *.gen.md（生成規則） 與審查 *.md（結構模板）。依文件管線順序、平行派送 Agent 執行，確保每份 review.md ≥ 20 項、≥ 4 層、有 Check/Risk/Fix 格式。 呼叫時機： - 新增文件類型後需同步建立 review.md - 審查標準老化需全量刷新 - 重大 pipeline 架構調整後重新驗證累積上游 ([ibalasite/gendoc](https://github.com/ibalasite/gendoc/tree/HEAD/skills/gendoc-rebuild-templates))
- **gendoc-refresh-docs**: 掃描目前目錄實況 + 讀取 Claude memory（project/reference 類型）+ git log， 與現有 README.md / docs/PRD.md 比對，找出過時或遺漏的內容並修正， 再將使用者 args（若有）優化後整合進去，最後選擇性重建 HTML。 適用任意目錄；不假設固定結構；根據 README/PRD 是否存在自動路由。 支援 args：/gendoc-refresh-docs &lt;文字&gt; 提供本次想補記的內容。 ([ibalasite/gendoc](https://github.com/ibalasite/gendoc/tree/HEAD/skills/gendoc-refresh-docs))
- **gendoc-repair**: 把不完整的目標專案補到與 gendoc-auto + gendoc-flow 從頭執行完全一致的狀態。三部分內容驅動 Gate：① pre-DRYRUN 文件品質（存在 + 章節數 ≥ template）② .gendoc-rules/*.json 存在 ③ post-DRYRUN 輸出存在性 — 三者全 ✅ 才進 Branch B，否則 Branch A 修補後重跑 DRYRUN。State 只作參考，不作 Gate 判斷依據。 ([ibalasite/gendoc](https://github.com/ibalasite/gendoc/tree/HEAD/skills/gendoc-repair))
- **gendoc-shared**: gendoc 共用邏輯執行入口。被 Skill tool 呼叫時，直接執行 §-1 R-01（state file 守衛）： 檢查 state file 是否存在，不存在則呼叫 gendoc-config 完整執行所有步驟。 ([ibalasite/gendoc](https://github.com/ibalasite/gendoc/tree/HEAD/skills/gendoc-shared))
- **gendoc-upgrade**: 手動升級 gendoc skills（git pull + re-deploy）。 呼叫時機：user 說「升級 gendoc」「gendoc 更新」「update gendoc」「/gendoc-upgrade」。 ([ibalasite/gendoc](https://github.com/ibalasite/gendoc/tree/HEAD/skills/gendoc-upgrade))
- **gene**: Manage the gene pool — compounding library of proven skill improvement strategies. Use when user asks to view genes, check gene pool, apply a gene, or /gene. ([Jack5316/Skills_Snapshot](https://github.com/Jack5316/Skills_Snapshot/tree/HEAD/gene))
- **gene**: Genetic profile advisor — health, learning, fitness questions from vault. ([Warren-Githut/personal-os-automation](https://github.com/Warren-Githut/personal-os-automation/tree/HEAD/personal_vault/.scripts/skills/gene) · 2 copies)
- **Gene Panel Selection Workflow**: End-to-end workflow for gene panel design in scRNA-seq and spatial transcriptomics, that should be **STRICTLY** followed: dataset understanding + smart downsampling + train/test splits, algorithmic selection (HVG/DE/RF/scGeneFit/SpaPROS), optimal sub-panel discovery (ARI vs size), biological completion with a stability gate (Completion Rule), consensus scoring and completion (only if there is still room), and benchmarking on test splits (ARI/NMI/Silhouette + UMAP similarity). ([aristoteleo/PantheonOS](https://github.com/aristoteleo/PantheonOS/tree/HEAD/pantheon/factory/templates/skills/omics/gene_panel_selection) · ★497 · 3 copies)
- **Gene Regulatory Network Inference (pySCENIC)**: Infer gene regulatory networks (GRNs) de novo from single-cell RNA-seq data using pySCENIC. This workflow discovers transcription factor (TF) regulons directly from expression patterns and calculates cell-level TF activity scores. ([BioTender-max/awesome-bio-agent-skills](https://github.com/BioTender-max/awesome-bio-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/omicsclaw/grn-pyscenic) · ★224 · 7 copies)
- **Gene Therapy Scientist Expert Agent**: Specialized expert agent operating as a Gene Therapy Scientist with full theoretical and practical domain reasoning. ([Tashima-Tarsh/Disha6.6](https://github.com/Tashima-Tarsh/Disha6.6/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/agent-gene-therapy-scientist))
- **gene-absence-confidence-statistics**: Use when a presence/absence analysis calls a gene absent in lineages represented mainly by draft bins — especially when completeness estimates (e.g. ≥75% single-copy genes, ≤1.125 copies) are available and you must decide if the absence call is trustworthy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/gene-absence-confidence-statistics))
- **gene-absence-presence-matrix-modeling**: Use when you have binary gene cluster absence/presence matrices plus literature-based lifestyle labels and need to train lifestyle classifiers or predict lifestyles of genomes from species with no representative in the training set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-absence-presence-matrix-modeling))
- **gene-abundance-and-prevalence-quantification**: Use when a set of clustered gene representatives must be prioritized by how abundant and how widespread they are across a defined collection of metagenomes, e.g. to select representative amoA-AOB clusters from soil metagenomes for primer design. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/gene-abundance-and-prevalence-quantification))
- **gene-accessibility-aggregation-from-atac-peaks**: Use when matched peak-level scATAC-Seq counts must be summarized to per-gene accessibility (3kb-upstream+gene-body aggregation around RNA-defined marker genes) to validate primed states or compare chromatin opening across cell groups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-accessibility-aggregation-from-atac-peaks))
- **gene-activity-score-integration**: Use when snRNA-seq and snATAC-seq come from unpaired samples and you need a shared low-dimensional representation before cell pairing or downstream GRN inference; the ATAC assay must be converted to a gene activity matrix. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-activity-score-integration))
- **gene-age-class-count-summarization**: Use when you already have a gene-to-gene-age-class orthomap table for a focal species (e.g. from oggmap of2orthomap on OrthoFinder output) and need per-age-class gene counts for quality checking or downstream plotting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-age-class-count-summarization))
- **gene-age-class-relative-expression-normalization**: Use when a TEI-annotated AnnData (h5ad) with gene age class annotations is available and you need a table of relative expression shares per gene age class per observation group (cell type, timepoint). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-age-class-relative-expression-normalization))
- **gene-age-orthomap-matching**: Use when an AnnData/scRNA object must be annotated with per-gene evolutionary ages (an orthomap) before computing a transcriptome evolutionary index (TEI), e.g. reproducing hourglass or young-gene-peak patterns across embryo stages. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-age-orthomap-matching))
- **gene-age-weighted-expression-analysis**: Use when single-cell RNA-seq AnnData objects need an evolutionary age metric (TEI or partial TEI profiles) per cell, especially for large datasets (~90,000+ up to 1e6 cells) where per-cell computation must stay tractable. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-age-weighted-expression-analysis))
- **gene-alias-resolution-against-hgnc**: Use when RNA-seq data are keyed by Ensembl gene IDs but a downstream biomolecular network model expects gene symbols, and direct symbol matches fail for some genes requiring GeneCards/HGNC alias lookup. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/gene-alias-resolution-against-hgnc))
- **gene-alteration-proportion-zscoring**: Use when building the CRIT feature matrix for candidate genes and you have per-sample TCGA mutation (data_mutations_extended.txt) and CNV (data_CNA.txt) files that must be dichotomized into per-gene alteration proportions and Z-scored. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-alteration-proportion-zscoring))
- **gene-annotation**: Use gget to discover Ensembl reference FTPs, search gene or transcript annotations, inspect Ensembl/WormBase/FlyBase IDs, and retrieve nucleotide or UniProt amino-acid FASTA sequences. ([VectorSpaceLab/AREX-Skill](https://github.com/VectorSpaceLab/AREX-Skill/tree/HEAD/skills/repositories/repo-skills/gget/sub-skills/gene-annotation) · ★357)
- **gene-annotation-column-preservation**: Use when mean-centering or otherwise transforming a COnTORT-style expression matrix where the first five columns are GenBank annotations and must survive transformation unchanged, including 'N/A' blanks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/gene-annotation-column-preservation))
- **gene-annotation-expression-matching**: Use when normalizing expression data from multiple GEO series for one organism: expression rows must be filtered to genes present in a GenBank annotation file and joined across experiments by locus tag/gene name. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/gene-annotation-expression-matching))
- **gene-annotation-gtf-processing**: Use when preparing a reference for splicing-status prediction of scRNA-seq reads: you have a gene annotation GTF and matching genome FASTA and need an augmented spliced+unspliced transcriptome (spliceu.gtf, spliceu.fa, t2g_3col.tsv). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-annotation-gtf-processing))
- **gene-annotation-overlap-classification-of-tars**: Use when you have groHMM-predicted TAR BED files from scRNA-seq alignments and need to partition them into aTARs vs uTARs relative to a refFlat annotation, or quantify the fraction of reads assigned to uTARs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/gene-annotation-overlap-classification-of-tars))
- **gene-annotation-strand-orientation-mapping**: Use when you have strand-annotated BED files of motifs (e.g. TFBSs) and gene regions and need to test whether motif orientation is biased relative to transcriptional direction within transcribed regions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/gene-annotation-strand-orientation-mapping))
